## TERM-DEDEKIND-INFINITE

- Source term or sense: Dedekind infinite / Dedekind algebra / successor function / closed under a function
- Chosen rendering or treatment: د ډېډېکېنډ له مخې نامتناهي / د ډېډېکېنډ الجبر / تالي تابع / د تابعې له مخې بند
- Locale/script: `ps-Arab-PK` / `Arab`
- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence
- Rationale: Dedekind infinite means that the set injects into a proper subset of itself. ## TERM-PROP-SYNTAX

- Source term or sense: propositional variable / logical connective / negation / conjunction / disjunction / conditional / biconditional / formation sequence
- Chosen rendering or treatment: بياني متغير / منطقي نښلوونکے / نفي / عطف / فصل / شرطي / دوه اړخيز شرطي / د فارمول جوړښتي لړ
- Locale/script: `ps-Arab-PK` / `Arab`
- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence
- Rationale: Use the connective labels consistently and preserve whether a connective is primitive or defined. A formation sequence may contain earlier formulas and even irrelevant entries; only its last entry is required to be the formula it forms.

## TERM-PROP-SEMANTICS

- Source term or sense: truth value / truth table / valuation / evaluation / satisfaction / tautology / semantic entailment / satisfiable / unsatisfiable / contingent
- Chosen rendering or treatment: د رښتياوالي قيمت / د رښتياوالي جدول / ارزښت ټاکنه / ارزونه / صدق / تاوتولوژي / معنايي استلزام / د صدق وړ / د صدق نۀ وړ / اتفاقي
- Locale/script: `ps-Arab-PK` / `Arab`
- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence
- Rationale: A valuation assigns truth values to propositional variables; evaluation extends that assignment to all formulas. Satisfaction is truth under a valuation. Tautologies are true under every valuation, contingent formulas under some but not all, and semantic entailment preserves satisfaction from premises to conclusion. Satisfiability requires at least one common satisfying valuation.
- Alternatives: صدق ټاکنه او د حقيقت جدول; اعتبار او مدلول
- Review request: Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «د رښتياوالي قيمت / د رښتياوالي جدول / ارزښت ټاکنه / ارزونه / صدق / تاوتولوژي / معنايي استلزام / د صدق وړ / د صدق نۀ وړ / اتفاقي» the clearest conventional rendering of “truth value / truth table / valuation / evaluation / satisfaction / tautology / semantic entailment / satisfiable / unsatisfiable / contingent” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-SUBSTITUTION

- Source term or sense: uniform substitution / simultaneous substitution
- Chosen rendering or treatment: يو شان ځايناستي / هممهاله ځايناستي
- Locale/script: `ps-Arab-PK` / `Arab`
- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence
- Rationale: Uniform substitution replaces every occurrence of one propositional variable by the same formula. Simultaneous substitution applies a listed family at once; do not read it as successive replacement unless a later definition explicitly fixes such an order.

## TERM-LOGIC-FORMS

- Source term or sense: formula / derivation / propositional logic / first-order logic / completeness
- Chosen rendering or treatment: فارمول / اشتقاق / بياني منطق / د لومړۍ درجې منطق / بشپړتيا
- Locale/script: `ps-Arab-PK` / `Arab`
- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence
- Rationale: A formula is an object-language expression; a derivation is a finite syntactic witness governed by a particular proof system. Completeness relates semantic consequence to derivability and must remain distinct from real-number completeness.
- Alternatives: صيغه د فارمول پر ځاے; استنتاج د اشتقاق پر ځاے

## TERM-PROOF-SYSTEMS

- Source term or sense: derivation system / derivation / theorem / derivable / inference rule / proof
- Chosen rendering or treatment: د اشتقاق نظام / اشتقاق / قضيه / د اشتقاق وړ / د استنتاج قاعده / ثبوت
- Locale/script: `ps-Arab-PK` / `Arab`
- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence
- Rationale: Use اشتقاق for the finite formal object and ثبوت for the broader mathematical proof or act of proving. A theorem has a derivation without undischarged external premises; derivability from Gamma depends on the selected system and must not be conflated with semantic entailment.
- Alternatives: د ثبوت نظام او صوري ثبوت; استدلالي حساب
- Review request: Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «د اشتقاق نظام / اشتقاق / قضيه / د اشتقاق وړ / د استنتاج قاعده / ثبوت» the clearest conventional rendering of “derivation system / derivation / theorem / derivable / inference rule / proof” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?

## TERM-PROOF-PROPERTIES

- Source term or sense: soundness / completeness / consistency / inconsistency / validity
- Chosen rendering or treatment: سموالے / بشپړتيا / سازګاري / ناسازګاري / اعتبار
- Locale/script: `ps-Arab-PK` / `Arab`
- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence
- Rationale: Soundness sends derivability to semantic consequence; completeness sends semantic consequence to derivability. Consistency is syntactic and satisfiability semantic, even when soundness and completeness later make the corresponding conditions equivalent.
- Alternatives: صحت او تکميل; نه تناقض او اعتبار
- Review request: Please double-check: In Pakistani Pashto mathematical prose, is «سموالے / بشپړتيا / سازګاري / ناسازګاري / اعتبار» the clearest conventional rendering of “soundness / completeness / consistency / inconsistency / validity” while preserving the distinctions in the rationale? If not, what exact replacement and inflected forms should be used?
- Timing: retrospective backfill from the pre-existing decision ledger; this records the then-current rationale and is not a claim about unrecorded contemporaneous motives

- Source term or sense: sequent / sequent calculus / natural deduction / assumption / discharge / tableau / signed formula / axiomatic derivation / modus ponens

## TERM-SEQUENT-STRUCTURE

- Source term or sense: antecedent / succedent / initial sequent / logical rule / structural rule / main operator
- Chosen rendering or treatment: مقدم / تالي / لومړنے سېکوېنټ / منطقي قاعده / جوړښتي قاعده / اصلي عملګر
- Locale/script: `ps-Arab-PK` / `Arab`
- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence
- Rationale: The antecedent is the finite sequence on the left and the succedent the finite sequence on the right. An initial sequent begins a derivation. Logical rules act on a displayed main operator, while structural rules rearrange, duplicate, remove or add sequence members without introducing a connective.
- Alternatives: مخکښ او وروستۍ برخه; ابتدايي سېکوېنټ او قاعدوي نښلون

## TERM-QUANTIFIER-RULES

- Source term or sense: universal quantifier / existential quantifier / closed term / free variable / bound variable
- Chosen rendering or treatment: عمومي سور / وجودي سور / تړلې اصطلاح / ازاد متغير / تړلے متغير
- Locale/script: `ps-Arab-PK` / `Arab`
- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence
- Rationale: A closed term contains no variables. The universal and existential rule schemas retain the source distinction between free occurrences in A(x) and the bound variable introduced by the quantifier; Pashto prose must not weaken those occurrence conditions.
- Alternatives: کلي او وجودي کميت ټاکونکي; تړلے ترم او ايګن متغير

## TERM-SEQUENT-RULES

- Source term or sense: eigenvariable / eigenvariable condition / weakening / contraction / exchange / cut
- Chosen rendering or treatment: ځانګړے متغير / د ځانګړي متغير شرط / کمزوري کول / انقباض / تبادله / پرېکون

- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence

## TERM-DERIVATION-SEARCH

- Source term or sense: end-sequent / topmost sequent / bottommost sequent / premise / conclusion / backward proof search / rule instance
- Chosen rendering or treatment: وروستنے سېکوېنټ / تر ټولو بره سېکوېنټ / تر ټولو لاندې سېکوېنټ / مقدمه / پايله / له پايلې څخه شاته د ثبوت لټون / د قاعدې کارونه

## TERM-DERIVABILITY-PROPERTIES

- Source term or sense: proof-theoretic / reflexivity / monotonicity / transitivity / compactness (finite witness)
- Chosen rendering or treatment: ثبوت-تيوريکي / انعکاسيت / يکنواختي / انتقاليت / د متناهي شاهد خاصيت

## TERM-SEQUENT-SOUNDNESS

- Source term or sense: sequent validity / sound derivation system / induction on derivation / lowest inference / semantic preservation
- Chosen rendering or treatment: د سېکوېنټ اعتبار / سم اشتقاقي نظام / پر اشتقاق استقرا / تر ټولو لاندې استنتاج / د معنايي اعتبار ساتنه
- Locale/script: `ps-Arab-PK` / `Arab`
- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence
- Rationale: Validity means that every interpretation satisfies the sequent. Soundness is proved by induction on derivation height or inference count, checking that each lowest rule preserves semantic validity from its premises to its conclusion.
- Alternatives: د سېکوېنټ صحت; د معنا ساتونکے استنتاج; پر ثبوت استقرا

## TERM-NATURAL-DEDUCTION

- Chosen rendering or treatment: فطري استنتاج / فرضيه / فرضيه ايستل / د داخلولو قاعده / د ايستلو قاعده / مقدمه / پايله

## TERM-TABLEAUX

- Source term or sense: tableau / signed formula / truth sign / branch / root / closed and open branch / cut / eigenvariable condition
- Chosen rendering or treatment: تابلو / نښه لرونکے فارمول / د رښتياوالي نښه / څانګه / ريښه / تړلې او پرانيستې څانګه / پرېکون / د ځانګړي متغير شرط
- Locale/script: `ps-Arab-PK` / `Arab`
- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence
- Rationale: A tableau is a downward-growing tree of signed formulas. A branch closes only when it contains matching true and false signs for the same formula. Branching rules create alternatives; nonbranching rules extend one path. Cut splits on an arbitrary signed formula, and quantified witness rules retain the exact closed-term and eigenvariable restrictions.
- Alternatives: رښتيا ونه; لاسليک لرونکے فارمول; خلاصه او بنده څانګه; د قضيو وېش

## TERM-COMPLETENESS-CONSTRUCTION

- Source term or sense: complete consistent set / saturated set / Henkin expansion / Lindenbaum lemma / term model / Truth Lemma
- Chosen rendering or treatment: بشپړ سازګار سټ / مشبوع سټ / د هنکين غځونه / د لينډنباوم لمه / ترمي مدل / د صدق لمه
- Locale/script: `ps-Arab-PK` / `Arab`
- Priority and confidence: high; provisional; best available evidence
- Rationale: A complete consistent set decides every sentence while remaining syntactically consistent. Saturation supplies quantified witnesses; Lindenbaum extension completes the set; the term model uses closed terms as its domain. The Truth Lemma equates satisfaction in that constructed model (or in the propositional valuation) with membership in the complete set. The compact Pashto labels are edition choices, and the cited Afghan logic is only a regional comparator.
- Alternatives: تام سازګار سټ; اشباع شوے سټ; د هنکين توسعه; د لينډنباوم فرعي قضيه; د اصطلاحاتو مدل; د رښتياوالي لمه
